- Read checks on the real BGB (L3 pass, 2026-10-14): `tcrf_check/tools/bgb_wp_probe.py CORPUS ENTRY WATCHPOINTS OUTPREFIX` replays one corpus entry in BGB (wine, headless) with *logging watchpoints* (`-wp BB:AAAA-BB:AAAA//r/NAME`: one line per hit in `debugmsg.txt`, the emulation goes on) and prints the hit counts, with a control watchpoint on bytes that certainly are read; `tcrf_check/tools/bgb_link_wp.py` does the same for two linked BGB instances (link-cable receiver watchpoints). Used for `02:46E6` and `04:4D09` (never executed, never read).
- Owner questions of the L3 pass (2026-10-14): `tcrf_check/tools/owner_page.py OUT.html` (template `owner_page.tpl.html`) builds the one page of what only ears or eyes can settle: 56 sound ids (the 95 SFX-tag rows merged per id, plus the two Space Fever II handlers of id 24), the 22 SHOOT effect variants (`06:60DB` -> `0A:541A`), 10 modes with generic state names, and five pictures (`0D:7000`, `0C:5800`, `14:4550`, the TCRF candidates `13:5A00` and `1A:7C80`). Answers are saved per item (confidence + note) and go to `disasm/owner_answers_jp.csv`; an answer moves the name from I to C. **Sound clips** (`tcrf_check/tools/sound_clips.py CHANNEL OUTDIR [ID...]`, results in `tcrf_check/assets/sound_clips/`, 90 MP3 of 32 kbit/s mono, 438 kB, `sound_clips_jp.csv`): BGB cannot post a sound request, so the ROM is patched in memory (the call to `Joy_ClearSticky` at `00:2F33` goes to a hook in the free bank-0 bytes at `$3600`, as in §16.26) and the hook writes the next id of a table to ONE request mailbox (`$DD60` / `$DD70` / `$DD78`) every N main-loop passes; its first pass asks `$FF` (reset) in the music mailbox `$DD68`, otherwise the main-menu music plays over everything. BGB records the audio (`WavFileOut=1`, 44.1 kHz) and logs the mailbox and NRx4 writes with `%TOTALCLKS%` (2,097,152 per second, 35,112 per frame). The WAV is lined up with the clock **by content** (the NRx4 triggers against the loud bins over a grid of offsets: -0.47 s here, not a constant of BGB), then each clip starts 10 ms before the first trigger after its request; the audible onset is within -6 to +9 ms of the first trigger for 85 of the 90 requests, which is the check that clip k is the sound of id k; the other five (`sfx1 25 26 27 33 34`, the ids that follow a still-sounding long effect) show loud bins 35-72 ms earlier, the tail of the previous effect, which the 10 ms lead keeps out of the clip. Gotchas: the hook counts main-loop passes (the main menu takes about 1.77 frames per pass, so the demo must be about 1.9 times longer than N x ids), the pass counter is one byte (N <= 255), and a window shorter than the sound cuts it (the ids `sfx1 24 25 26 32 33` are still sounding after 6 s: 6 s excerpts, flagged in the csv; `14 2F 30` come from the long run). These are BGB's APU output: nobody listened, and BGB is not a real Game Boy. The owner page plays them (`owner_page.py` embeds the MP3 as base64 and decodes them with WebAudio).
- Open assets: `tcrf_check/tools/unidentified_sheets.py` renders every asset row that was open in `disasm/sym_unconclusive_jp.csv` when the sheet was written as one sheet `tcrf_check/assets/unidentified/open_assets.png` (154 rows, 24,249 B after §16.25; 161 rows, 30,877 B after the §16.24 relabel and the `11:5800` split; 341 rows, 73,278 B in §16.23); all rows with their byte pattern and overlapping TCRF ids are in `open_assets.csv`
- `disasm/code_gaps.md` — full report behind §1.2 (resolved indirect sites, verdict on the unreferenced regions)
- `HOWTO_NEW_ROM.md` — playbook for disassembling the next ROM (levels L0-L6, stage order, tool list, pitfalls, what was not done here), written from this project; condensed as a skill in `skills/gb-rom-disassembly/SKILL.md`
- `pocketcamera_jp.sym` — the complete symbol file (every byte typed and every routine / asset named, §1.1.1), generated by `tools/sym_build.py` from `disasm/sym_names_jp.csv` and `disasm/sym_types_jp.csv`; `tools/sym_fix.py` (readability pass over the mgbdis output), `tools/make_doubts.py` and `tools/bank_pics.py` (the doubt list `disasm/sym_unconclusive_jp.md` / `.csv` and its bank pictures); `tools/check_repo_consistency.py --rom ROM [--roundtrip]` (every number the docs quote against the file it comes from, every generated file against a fresh run, the quoted `§` numbers and asset labels, the md5 round trip: run it before every commit)
- `disasm/ram_names_jp.csv` — the RAM names (`addr,size,name,scope,tag,desc`; 788 rows, §2.13) and `disasm/ram_exclude_jp.csv` (the pointer loads of the access table that are plain numbers); `tools/ram_names.py` generates `disasm/ram_jp.inc` (`DEF wMode EQU $d5ce`, `INCLUDE`d by the regenerated `game.asm`) and `pocketcamera_jp_ram.sym` (the same names for BGB / no$gba); `tools/sym_fix.py --ram` applies them
- `disasm/renamed_jp.csv` — every label change of the L3 pass of 2026-10-14 (`bank,addr,old_name,new_name,old_conf,new_conf,reason`; 470 rows: renames, evidence upgrades, removed labels, new labels).

### Mode table (`$2F3F`)

The 34-entry mode table (`$00-$21`) with the role of every mode is in §2.3.

Extra tool added with the Select+Start+Up work: `tools/render_state10.py ROM SAV OUT.png [scale]`
(composes the erase-all confirmation background from ROM tiles and a real save's thumbnails).
Emulator test scripts (PyBoy, MBC5 stand-in, patched copy only) are described in §8.
